Structural, Chemical & Synthetic Biology
We utilise a transdisciplinary approach to understand biomolecular function, integrating structural biology, chemical biology, and synthetic biology. This expertise advances biology and medicine, enabling innovations in therapeutics, diagnostics, and bioengineering to address complex biological and medical challenges.
Structural, Chemical and Synthetic Biology - A transdisciplinary understanding of biomolecule function at the atomic level provides insights into the molecular biology underlying all of life and medicine. We are now moving from observations of the structure and dynamics of molecular complexes and machines (structural biology), towards perturbing these systems (chemical biology), through to re-engineering biomolecules by both rational design and evolution (synthetic biology). Our School has deep expertise in these areas - integrating structural biology with functional proteomics, nucleic acid evolution with translational technology, and artificial biological circuits with genome engineering. Such approaches are allowing new ways to better understand biology and medicine, and allow translational angles across therapeutics, diagnostics and bioengineering.
– Clive Chung

Technology Transfer & Innovation
Our School’s Technology Transfer & Innovation initiatives, led by star PIs in research and industry, drive digital health and biomedical advancements. Through strategic leadership at HKU, HKUMed, and industry collaborations, it fosters translational research, supports start-up development, and enhances our global reputation in biomedical innovation.
Our School’s Technology Transfer & Innovation initiatives, led by star PIs in research and industry, drive digital health and biomedical advancements. Through strategic leadership at HKU, HKUMed, and industry collaborations, it fosters translational research, supports start-up development, and enhances our global reputation in biomedical innovation.
– Centre for Virology, Vaccinology and Therapeutics
– Centre for Oncology and Immunology
– Centre for Translational Stem Cell Biology
– HKUMed Technology Transfer Unit

Admission Requirements
Why Study Here?Admission RequirementsApplication ProceduresScholarshipsFeesNon-local StudentsAdministrative Contact and InquiriesOther Collaborations
The Department of Chemistry offers the following programs and degrees:
M.Phil (2-year)
Ph.D. (3-year)
Ph. D. (4-year)
Admission Requirements for Dept of Chemistry
A B. Sc. degree with IIA honours or above from this University or an equivalent qualification from a comparable institution.
An M. Phil. (Research) degree from this University or an equivalent qualification from a comparable institution.
A B. Sc. degree with 1st Honours from this University or an equivalent qualification from a comparable institution.
Program
Research; ethics course, compulsory and elective postgraduate courses
Research; ethics course, safety course and other postgraduate courses as stipulated by Department/Supervisor
Research, ethics course, compulsory and elective postgraduate courses
Remarks
Students accepted to read for the M. Phil. can apply to transfer to the 4-year Ph. D. program after successfully completing the first year of studies
--
--
In addition, if your B. Sc. is obtained from a university or an institution outside Hong Kong where the language of teaching and/or examination is not English, you will also need to fulfill an English requirement.
Departmental Requirements
For MPhil students: Participation in and presentation at research seminars and/or guided study.
For PhD students: Participation in and presentation at seminars; any other specific departmental requirements.

Why Study Here?Admission RequirementsApplication ProceduresScholarshipsFeesNon-local StudentsAdministrative Contact and InquiriesOther Collaborations
Application Procedures
Applications to the M. Phil. and Ph. D. program in the Department of Chemistry are handled centrally by the University of Hong Kong Graduate School.
You can make application on-line.
For the on-line application, you should have ready your certificates and complete transcripts of your undergraduate and postgraduate studies which will need to be uploaded via the on-line application system. An application fee of $600 applies and can be paid by credit card.
Application Period: https://gradsch.hku.hk/prospective_students/application/application_period
Supporting Documents: https://gradsch.hku.hk/prospective_students/application/supporting_documents
FAQ: https://gradsch.hku.hk/prospective_students/application/faq_on_application
Area of interest and potential supervisor
Before application, you are strongly encouraged to explore the research interests of our Research Supervisors and to communicate with one or more potential supervisors, who will be able to discuss further details about their research programs and their projected number of RPg intakes in the upcoming academic year.
A research proposal must also be written up for the application, the template for this is here.
For further application procedures and details, please click here.

Research Postgraduates (PhD/MPhil)
General Information
GENERAL INFORMATION
Departmental Requirements
For MPhil students: Participation in and presentation at research seminars and guided study.
For PhD students: Participation in and presentation at seminars; any other specific departmental requirements.
Postgraduate Admissions Advisor
Professor L.N.Y. Wong
Email: lnywong@hku.hk

Research Postgraduates (PhD/MPhil)
How to Apply
HOW TO APPLY
Please visit the Graduate School website of the University of Hong Kong (http://www.gradsch.hku.hk) for information on application requirements and procedures. Your application will need to include a research proposal to demonstrate your research focus and capability. It is essential for prospective students to contact potential supervisor(s) ahead of application submission in order to determine if he/she/they are interested in admitting a new student and to discuss project details. Making timely contact with a potential supervisor ensures that your application is complete and will save you time if the staff member(s) concerned have no vacancies.
Copyright © The University of Hong Kong, Department of Earth & Planetary Sciences. Powered by
ANGLIA.

Research Postgraduates (PhD/MPhil)
Fellowships
FELLOWSHIPS
A formally registered research student will have full financial support during his/her study at the University of Hong Kong. Candidates with strong academic backgrounds are urged to apply for the Hong Kong PhD Fellowship (HKPF) or the University Postgraduate Fellowship (UPF).
Copyright © The University of Hong Kong, Department of Earth & Planetary Sciences. Powered by
ANGLIA.

General Information
Excellence in Research and Teaching Booklet 2026
The following M.Phil./Ph.D. projects are available in 2026/2027 academic year. Students are encouraged to contact their prospective supervisors directly to obtain the further detailed information of the project. We also welcome students to visit our laboratories and research facilities.
Full-time MPhil and PhD students who hold a first degree with second-class honours first division (or equivalent) or above are normally considered eligible to receive a Postgraduate Scholarship (HK$19,135 per month) during the normative study period. This year we expect to admit a large number of postgraduate students. Students please visit the homepage of HKU graduate school at https://gradsch.hku.hk/gradsch/ and get the information as well as application forms there.
For other details, please contact Prof. S.Z. Zhang (Tel. 3917 7943, email address: phys-student@hku.hk), Department of Physics, The University of Hong Kong, Pokfulam Road, Hong Kong.

Studying RPg at HKU Science
Join Us
JOIN US
Are you inquisitive?
Are you good at logic and numbers?
Do you want to understand the law of everything?
Do you want to identify pressing problems and solve challenges?
Poster:
Leaflet:
The Faculty of Science aspires to pursue research eminence, and our six Science Departments and five interdisciplinary research units are now in position to nurture our research strengths.
We have one of the world’s premier science faculties here, with around 150 renowned scientists in a broad spectrum of research fields, ranging from natural sciences to mathematical and statistical sciences. Our publications are at the forefront of scientific research, and 20.8% of the professoriate staff in our Science departments are classified top 1% scholars in average over the past decade in the Clarivate Analytics' Essential Science Indicators.
Striving to achieve breakthroughs in scientific research and make profound impact on mankind, we also collaborate extensively with world-class universities in interdisciplinary research projects, with the support of cutting-edge research facilities and robust funding.
We are now inviting high calibre students for PhD and MPhil studies to join us.
Video of admissions talk to postgraduate studies
